# Cloud Studio IoT

> Monitor, automate, and grow your operation from a single IoT solution without the complexity

## About

Cloud Studio IoT is a low-code, white-label, multi-tenant IoT Application Enablement Platform (AEP).
It enables organizations to monitor, automate, and scale IoT operations without building from scratch.

- **Products**: CS Gear (platform), CS Link (connectivity), CS Sense (devices)
- **Deployment**: Cloud on AWS (Frankfurt for European customers, the US for the rest of the world) or on-premise
- **Protocols**: MQTT, HTTPS and TCP, LoRaWAN through external network servers (The Things Stack, Actility ThingPark, LORIOT, ChirpStack, Helium, IoT Creators, Orbiwise), NB-IoT, GPS, and Modbus or OPC UA through a software gateway installed at the edge; Sigfox, Mioty, CoAP and other protocols are integrated on request
- **Industries**: 30+ verticals including smart cities, agriculture, energy, logistics, industrial
- **White-Label**: 3-level branding (instance, client, end-user)
- **Scale**: 150,000+ devices deployed, 15+ countries
- **Pricing**: Per-instance flat pricing starting at EUR 100/month

## Key Capabilities

- Real-time monitoring with customizable dashboards and 20+ widget types
- Automated alerts with 15+ alarm types, sent by email, SMS, WhatsApp, voice call and push
- JavaScript scripting engine for custom business logic
- Multi-tenant isolation with unlimited tenants per instance
- Native mobile app for iOS and Android (Gear Studio)
- Connects to FIWARE over MQTT or HTTP (NGSI-LD is not native)
- Device driver support for 50+ sensor models from 17+ manufacturers

## Success Cases

- [Buenos Aires Smart Lighting](https://cloudstudioiot.com/success-cases): about 150,000 street lights managed and monitored with partner Smartmation
- [MoviTHERM Early Fire Detection](https://cloudstudioiot.com/success-cases): thermal cameras integrated for early fire detection at industrial sites
- [Canary Islands Schools](https://cloudstudioiot.com/success-cases): 240 solar-powered 4G sensors in 120 schools on 7 islands, installed in 18 working days
- [Heritage Preservation Tarragona](https://cloudstudioiot.com/success-cases): Protecting 2,000-year-old Roman Necropolis
- [Bahia Principe Water Management](https://cloudstudioiot.com/success-cases): 25% water savings, 1,000+ smart meters

- [Fundamental Concepts](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/conceptos-fundamentales): This is where we'll break down the key terms that will make you a master of our platform. We know you're already an expert, but even geniuses need a solid foundation.
- [Quick Start](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs): Getting started with Gear Studio, the Cloud Studio IoT platform: what it is, how it is organized and where to begin connecting devices.
- [Requirements and Best Practices](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/requisitos-y-buenas-practicas): Minimum requirements to install the platform on premises: 4 vCPUs, 16 GB of RAM, 500 GB of disk, Windows Server and SQL Server 2019.
- [Persistent Access Tokens](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/apis-de-extraccion-de-datos/access-tokens-persistentes): How to get a persistent access token with administrator permissions and a set lifetime to integrate external systems with the platform.
- [Instance Mapping API](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/apis-de-extraccion-de-datos/api-de-mapeo-de-instancia): The instance mapping API returns IDs and descriptions of clients, facilities, devices and endpoints, up to 500 records per call.
- [Data Extraction APIs](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/apis-de-extraccion-de-datos): How to extract data from Gear Studio with the HTTP API: alerts, alarms, geozones and endpoint data, with filters and incremental queries.
- [Low-Code Tools (Scripting)](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting): What platform scripts are: JavaScript code to process device data, run complex actions and define devices with no native support.
- [Additional Features](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/caracteristicas-adicionales): Advanced features you can request to extend the platform, such as instance-level white labeling, and how to request them.
- [Clients](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/clientes): How to create, edit and delete clients from the instance's global configuration, and how to open each client's own settings.
- [Global Configuration](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global): The following sections present tutorials for the configurations offered by the Cloud Studio platform at the instance level. This section will be available only to environment administrators.
- [General Parameters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/parametros-generales): From this section you can define and modify general parameters. This parameterization will apply to all existing clients within the instance in question.

Enabling the facility map To enable this feature and display the facility screen in the monitor, you need to configure the client permission by checking the "Enable facility map" option as shown in the following image.

This enables fast updates of alarms as they are opened or closed without needing to retrieve the full list.

This enables fast updates of alerts as they are created, modified, or deleted, without needing to retrieve the full list.

- [Device address validation result](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/device-address-validation-result): The device address validation result object represents the result of a device address validation, typically used in device model configuration scripts.

- [Device UI rules](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/device-ui-rules): The device UI rules object represents the user interface rules applied to a device, typically used in device model configuration scripts.
- [Device](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/device): Reference for the device scripting object: the device that data conversion scripts receive and the one read from actions.
- [Endpoint collection](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-collection): Reference for the endpoint collection scripting object: a device's collection of endpoints, its count and how to iterate over it.
- [Endpoint configuration collection](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-configuration-collection): The endpoint configuration collection object represents a collection of endpoints for which initial configuration is to be established, typically in device model configuration scripts.
- [Endpoint configuration](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-configuration): Reference for the endpoint configuration object: an endpoint's initial setup in device model scripts, created with the add() method.
- [Endpoint range](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-range): Reference for the endpoint range scripting object: the acceptable range of values for an endpoint, with optional minimum and maximum.
- [Endpoint Scripting Utils](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-scripting-utils): Reference for Endpoint Scripting Utils: methods such as getDataPoints to read an endpoint's states from a given date in your scripts.
- [Endpoint UI rules](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint-ui-rules): The endpoint UI rules object represents the user interface rules applied to a device, typically used in device model configuration scripts.
- [Environment](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/environment): Reference for the global Environment object, available as env in every script, with basic functions such as log() for debugging.
- [HttpResponse](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/httpresponse): Reference for the HttpResponse scripting object: the status code and content returned when a device sends data over HTTP.
- [Scripting Object Reference](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting): This section contains information about the objects available for scripting. See the sub-sections for more information about each object type.
- [Multi-language literal](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/multi-language-literal): Reference for the multi-language literal scripting object: error or information messages written in several languages at once.
- [RSSI status](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/rssi-status): Reference for the RSSI status scripting object: a device's wireless signal level, updated with updateDeviceRssi.

- [Add Global Script](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales/agregar-script-global): Select the Common Scripts option from the menu. When selecting Add, the user can include a description, select a dependency, and enter the JS code below.
- [Edit Global Script](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales/editar-script-global): In the Common Scripts general section, select the three dots on the right side of the screen.
- [Delete Global Script](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales/eliminar-script-global): How to delete a global common script from the three-dot menu of the section, with a confirmation step before it is removed.
- [Global Common Scripts](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales): The following module allows working with "Global Common Scripts" for all clients, to reuse, simplify, and reduce the code of Device and Action Scripts.
- [Global Groups](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global/grupos-globales): Global groups assign permissions in bulk: global permissions are attached to the group and every user added to it inherits them.
- [Global Security](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global): Within "Global Configuration" in the Manager panel, you will find the Global Security option. Here you can add global users, edit them, set passwords, delete them, and also suspend them.
- [Create Dashboards](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/crear-dashboards): To create a new dashboard, navigate to the Dashboards menu in the Monitor and press the Add Dashboard button. The user can add a Description and Comments in the Details tab as needed.
- [Create Groups and Widgets](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/crear-grupos-y-widgets): How to add groups and widgets to a dashboard, and which predefined widgets the platform includes: active alarms, counters, energy and more.
- [Edit Groups and Widgets](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/editar-grupos-y-widgets): Dashboard Design editing is tied to each user's permissions. If the user has the required permission, they can use the edit button located in the upper right corner of the dashboards when entering the Dashboards option in the Monitor menu.
- [Filters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/filtros): The user can use the filter icon to perform a specific search within a defined time period, in order to obtain the data that devices recorded during the selected dates.
- [Dashboards](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards): A Dashboard is a graphical screen designed to present data and information in a visual, quick, and clear manner. Dashboards help users make data-driven decisions from multiple sources.
- [Dashboard List](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/lista-de-dashboards): From this section, the user can manage all dashboards they have created. From the Dashboards option and selecting the icon shown below, the dashboard list can be accessed.
- [Time Period Selection](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/seleccion-de-periodos-de-tiempo): Introduction The platform allows time period selection in various situations, such as: Dashboards Widgets Historical data visualization Reports
- [Export reports as CSV using the facility-specific separator](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/reportes/exportar-reportes-como-csv-usando-el-separador-correspondiente-al-facility): This section allows exporting the alarm history to a Microsoft Excel document.
- [Reports](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/reportes): In the Reports section, the user can view different types of options from which to download a report. The available reports for viewing and downloading are listed below.
- [Notification List](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/reportes/listado-de-notificaciones): Introduction The notification list allows viewing the report filtered by Creation date, Facility, notification type, channel, and Client. An administrator with a global user can filter by multiple clients. The platform allows downloading the report in PDF/Excel.
- [Report Export Customization](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/reportes/personalizacion-de-exportacion-de-reportes): This feature allows customizing the subject and body of the email sent when scheduling a report. Additionally, it allows adjusting the name of the attached document, the header, and the footer.
- [Configured Notifications Report by Instance](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/reportes/reporte-de-notificaciones-configuradas-por-instancia): Report of the notifications configured across the whole instance, filtered by client, facility, channel and recipient.
- [Operable Endpoints](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/vistas/endpoints-operables): The following table details the endpoint types that allow operation, meaning those endpoint types that support updating an endpoint's state from a view.
- [Endpoint States with Image Associated to Discrete Variables](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/vistas/estados-de-endpoints-con-imagen-asociado-a-variables-discretas): It is possible to associate discrete variables to the states of a Custom Endpoint, to subsequently associate those Endpoints to the Endpoint status image element. If no image exists for a value, a default image will be displayed.
- [Views](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/vistas): Views allow designing SCADA visualizations where images can be inserted and then overlaid with data that, unlike what can be achieved with dashboards, updates in near real-time.
- [Triggers](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/disparadores): Triggers can be created for actions based on any event, including Calendar and State events. For each trigger, the user interface typically offers two options.
- [Action Execution](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/ejecucion-de-acciones): Action execution is based on steps, and the set of these constitutes all the activities that are triggered when the action runs, regardless of whether the action is started manually or by any of its triggers.
- [Actions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones): Actions are sets of steps that can be executed manually or as a consequence of configured events. Once an action starts, all associated steps are executed one after another in the established order until the sequence is completed.
- [Map Configuration](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente/configuracion-de-mapas): How to set a client's minimum map radius: the distance in meters the map covers toward each cardinal direction.
- [Client](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente): How to manage a client in Gear Studio: create and edit it, and set options such as maps and terms and conditions.
- [Terms and Conditions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente/terminos-y-condiciones): How to define terms and conditions per client, which every user must accept before using that client's applications.
- [Composite Alerts (AND/OR)](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alertas-compuestas): How to combine up to 5 conditions with AND or OR in one alert, how the engine evaluates them and what limits composite alerts have.
- [Alerts](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alertas): How to set up an alert on an endpoint: alert type, threshold, minimum duration and contacts, so alarms are raised when values go out of range.
- [Configuring Contacts for Notifications](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/configuracion-contactos-para-notificaciones): Who receives an alert's notifications: address book contacts and groups, individual email addresses, and phone numbers for SMS and calls.
- [Alerts and Alarms](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas): The difference between alerts and alarms in Gear Studio: alerts set thresholds on a variable and generate alarms when they are crossed.
- [Alarm Severity](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/severidad-de-alarmas): Alarm severity levels, low, medium or high, how they are set from the alert, and which value an alarm takes by default.
- [Variables for Alert Notifications](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/variables-para-notificaciones-de-alertas): Variables such as {CLIENT_NAME}, {FACILITY_NAME} or {DEVICE_NAME} that put client, facility and device data into alert notification messages.
- [Facilities](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones): What a facility is in Gear Studio: the physical site, such as a factory, building or warehouse, where devices and gateways are deployed.
- [Facilities](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/instalaciones): How to create, edit and delete facilities in Gear Studio, and what else you can configure for each one in the facilities section.
- [Maintenance Mode](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/modo-mantenimiento): How to put a facility in Maintenance Mode to pause alarms and automation rules during repairs while sensor data keeps coming in.
- [Sectors](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/sectores): What sectors are within a facility in Gear Studio, and how to set up automation rules that apply to each of those areas on its own.
- [Facility Types](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/tipos-de-instalacion): How to use facility types to group and compare data by the kind of site, such as factories, buildings or warehouses.
- [Devices and Endpoints](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints): How Gear Studio organizes each facility's infrastructure: physical devices, such as sensors or gateways, and their endpoints.
- [Create a New Contact](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/crear-un-nuevo-contacto): How to create a new contact in the Gear Studio address book with the Add button, even while the search filter is active.
- [Create a New Contact Group](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/crear-un-nuevo-grupo-de-contacto): How to create a contact group in the Gear Studio address book: the group name and its contacts, ready to use in alerts.
- [Edit a Contact](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/editar-un-contacto): How to edit or delete a contact in the Gear Studio address book from its three-dot menu, without leaving required fields empty.
- [Edit a Contact Group](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/editar-un-grupo-de-contactos): How to edit or delete a contact group in the address book from its three-dot menu, without leaving the name empty.
- [Address Book](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones): The address book holds the contacts for SMS, email and voice notifications: name, company, position, email and phone numbers.
- [Security](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/seguridad): Within "Client Configuration" in the Manager panel, you will find the Security option. Here you can add users, edit them, set a password, delete them, and also suspend them.
- [Verticals](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales): Ready-to-use verticals in Gear Studio built on the most common use cases: energy monitoring, tank monitoring and asset tracking.
- [Tank Monitoring](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales/monitoreo-de-tanques): The tank monitoring vertical shows contents, temperature and volume in real time, with alarms to catch failures early.
- [Get endpoint data incrementally](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/apis-de-extraccion-de-datos/datos-de-endpoints/obtener-datos-de-endpoints-en-forma-incremental): This API allows retrieving a list of Endpoints incrementally. This enables fast updates of Endpoints without needing to retrieve the full list.
- [Endpoint](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint): The endpoint object represents an endpoint within a device installed in the platform. Endpoints are normally accessed through the endpoints property of the device object.
- [Global Permissions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global/permisos-globales): Cloud Studio has a global permissions system that allows establishing, for each user or group of users, the set of functionalities they have access to at the instance level.
- [Global Users](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global/usuarios-globales): Global users can have access to configuration options at the instance and client level. They can also belong to one or more global groups that have associated global permissions.
- [Share Dashboard - Mobile App](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/compartir-dashboard/compartir-dashboard-app-mobile): The user can use the corresponding icon to share the Dashboard. When accessing the Dashboard from the Dashboard List and editing is required, the Share option will not be enabled until edit mode is closed.
- [Share Dashboard](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/compartir-dashboard): The user can use the corresponding icon to share the Dashboard and/or download it in two formats. When accessing the Dashboard from the Dashboard List and editing is required, the Share option will not be enabled until edit mode is closed.
- [Metrics](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/metricas): A metric is a quantitative measure used to evaluate and monitor the performance of an IoT system or device in real time. Metrics are used to collect data that can be analyzed to gain valuable insights into the behavior and effectiveness of the IoT device.
- [Widgets](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets): The Cloud Studio platform features a series of specific widgets for branch monitoring, energy consumption, power history, consumption, weather data, and more, for use in dashboards customizable by the end user.
- [Dynamic Widget Titles](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/titulos-dinamicos-de-widgets): Widgets with dynamic titles allow customizing the information displayed on the dashboard by using variables such as {facility_desc}, {device_desc}, and {endpoint_desc}. To use them, include them in the "Title" field when creating your widget and check the "Title" checkbox to enable this feature.
- [Device Widget](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/widget-de-dispositivos): The user can use this Widget to view relevant information about a specific device, as well as data from up to 2 of its endpoints.

The Endpoint status image element will display the preconfigured image based on the state reported by the endpoint.
- [Endpoint Status Text](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/endpoint-status-text): From the Views section in the Monitor panel, the user can view the states of a discrete or scalar variable associated with an endpoint. The Endpoint status text element will display the preconfigured value based on the state reported by the endpoint.

- [Devices](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos): Devices are the first level of a facility's infrastructure. They typically correspond to physical devices such as sensors, gateways, dimmers, actuators, thermostats, etc.
- [Create an endpoint](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/crear-un-endpoint): IMPORTANT: as a general rule, endpoints can only be created on devices that correspond to user-defined device models. This is because when creating devices that correspond to models built into Gear Studio, the platform automatically creates all necessary endpoints.

- [Endpoints](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints): A device can have multiple sensors, functions, or channels. For example, a dimmer capable of controlling four light circuits can be said to have four distinct functions or "channels". When a user interacts with the device, they are actually interacting with one of those channels, not the entire device.

- [History - Granularity](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-granularidad): State granularity is a feature that allows users to adjust the level of detail at which device state measurements are presented. This control over granularity provides crucial flexibility for monitoring, as users can choose how data is presented based on the context and analysis needs.
- [History - Grid](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-grilla): The platform includes predefined widgets that facilitate data presentation in dashboards. Among them are the history and comparative history widgets. They allow viewing the evolution of Endpoint measurements over time.
- [History - Disconnection Time](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-tiempo-de-desconexion): There are occasions when a device disconnects but measurements continue to be generated. When the device reconnects, the stored measurements from that device are automatically synchronized with the system, allowing the user to see the complete data sequence without manual intervention.
- [History](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos): Line chart showing the variation of an endpoint variable type over time. In endpoint history charts, the user can enter minimum and maximum values to define the Y-axis ranges, as well as modify the variable names associated with the Y-axis titles.

This tool is especially useful for large-scale installations, as it avoids manual entry one by one, even allowing you to combine different device models in a single file.

This feature allows promoting a local device model to a global model, with the goal of reusing configurations across different clients.

- [Menu Options](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/real-time-log-broker/opciones-de-menu): Once the session is open, the user can access the following functions through the control panel. - [Clone Variable Types](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/clonar-tipos-de-variables): Introduction Variables allow us to define and determine counts or measurements of multiple states, such as temperature, time, occupancy, people flow, among others. Due to the diverse uses of variables within the platform, variable cloning was created.

There are certain standard variable types that are defined by default on the platform such as temperature, humidity, and pressure.
- [Local Variable Promotion](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/promocion-de-variables-locales): On the platform, variables can be defined at the client level (local) or at the global level (shared by all clients in an instance). This feature allows promoting a local variable to a global variable, facilitating its reuse across multiple contexts within the platform.
- [Global Variable Replacement](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/reemplazo-de-variable-global): This is the process by which a local variable (defined by a client) is removed and replaced by a global variable, applying this change across all devices, models, or environments where it was previously configured.

- [Flexible Data Exchange](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/intercambio-de-datos-flexible): Introduction Flexible data exchange is the recommended MQTT integration method on the Gear Studio platform. What are expressions? Expressions are texts that allow evaluating data, performing calculations, and returning a single value.

- [Data Formats](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/formatos-de-datos): When using the APIs via HTTP and MQTT, certain data formats must be followed, as described below.
- [Functions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/funciones): Functions allow obtaining values through the transformation of others. The following is a list of functions divided into categories, according to their typical use.
- [Operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores): Operators allow creating expressions by modifying or calculating values from others, known as "operands". Depending on the type of operation to perform, and/or the data type they apply to, operators can be classified into different categories.
- [Arithmetic operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-aritmeticos): Arithmetic operators are applied in mathematical operations, and the result of their application is always a number.
- [Logical operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-logicos): Logical operators are applied in logical operations, and the result of their application is always a boolean value (true / false).
- [String operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-para-strings): String operators are applied to character strings, and the result of their application is always a string value.
- [Relational operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-relacionales): Relational operators are applied in comparison operations, and the result of their application is always a boolean value (true / false).
- [Update RSSI Status and Battery Level](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/actualizar-estado-de-rrsi-y-nivel-de-bateria): How to report a device's RSSI and battery level over MQTT with the HTTP bridge. The platform keeps only the latest value, with no history.
- [Update Device Location](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/actualizar-ubicacion-de-un-dispositivo): How to report a device's geographic location over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [Appliances and Other On/Off Devices](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/appliances-y-otros-dispositivos-on-off): How to send the on/off state of valves, lamps, motors and other devices to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [People Counters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/contadores-de-personas): How to send people counts from a people counter to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [Curtain and Closure Controllers](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/controladores-de-cortinas-y-cerramientos): How to send the position and state of a curtain or closure controller to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [Dimmers](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/dimmers): How to send the level of a dimmer or speed controller to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [Frequency Meters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/frecuencimetros): How to send readings in hertz from a frequency meter to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [HVAC / Thermostats](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/hvac-termostatos): How to send the status of an HVAC unit or thermostat to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.
- [HTTP Bridge](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http): Introduction The HTTP bridge is a feature of the Gear Studio platform that allows device integration using the HTTP API through MQTT. This makes it possible to migrate devices that use the HTTP interface to use MQTT instead, with minimal changes.
- [Location Trackers](https://cloudstudioiot.com/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/puente-http/rastreadores-de-ubicacion): How to send the position of a location tracker to Gear Studio over MQTT with the HTTP bridge: message structure and parameters.

- [Marca blanca](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-global/marca-blanca): Cómo personalizar la plataforma con tu marca: logo en menú, reportes, notificaciones y login, paleta de colores y fondo de la pantalla de acceso.
- [Marca Blanca - Nivel Usuario](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-global/marca-blanca/marca-blanca-nivel-usuario): Cómo cada usuario puede cambiar logo, colores de fondo y texto de la interfaz a su gusto. Los cambios solo se aplican a su propia sesión.
- [Agregar Script Global](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales/agregar-script-global): Cómo agregar un script común global: descripción, dependencia y el código JavaScript que luego pueden reutilizar todos los clientes.

- [Eliminar Script Global](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-global/scripting-comunes-globales/eliminar-script-global): Cómo eliminar un script común global desde el menú de tres puntos de la sección, con confirmación antes de borrarlo.

- [Seguridad Global](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global): Cómo administrar usuarios globales desde la configuración global del Manager: crearlos, editarlos, asignar contraseñas, suspenderlos y borrarlos.

- [Filtros](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/monitor/dashboards/filtros): Cómo usar el filtro de fechas de un dashboard para ver los datos que registraron los dispositivos en el período elegido.
- [Dashboards](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/monitor/dashboards): Qué es un dashboard en Cloud Studio IoT y qué widgets ofrece: métricas en tiempo real, históricos, consumo de energía, clima y alarmas.
- [Lista de Dashboards](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/monitor/dashboards/lista-de-dashboards): Desde la lista de dashboards se crean, editan y eliminan los dashboards del usuario. Cómo acceder a ella y qué acciones ofrece.

- [Vistas](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/monitor/vistas): Las vistas permiten diseñar pantallas tipo SCADA: una imagen de fondo con datos de sensores superpuestos que se actualizan casi en tiempo real.
- [Disparadores](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/disparadores): Cómo configurar disparadores para una acción: por calendario, eligiendo días y hora, o por estado, cuando un endpoint cambia de valor.
- [Ejecución de Acciones](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/ejecucion-de-acciones): Cómo se ejecuta una acción: sus pasos corren en orden, uno tras otro, tanto si se inicia a mano como si la lanza uno de sus disparadores.
- [Acciones](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones): Las acciones son secuencias de pasos que se ejecutan a mano o ante un evento configurado, como un horario o un cambio de estado de un endpoint.
- [Configuración de mapas](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente/configuracion-de-mapas): Cómo configurar el radio mínimo de los mapas de un cliente: la distancia en metros que abarca el mapa hacia cada punto cardinal.

- [Severidad de alarmas](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/severidad-de-alarmas): Niveles de severidad de las alarmas, baja, media o alta, cómo se asignan desde la alerta y qué valor toman por defecto.
- [Variables para notificaciones de alertas](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/variables-para-notificaciones-de-alertas): Variables como {CLIENT_NAME}, {FACILITY_NAME} o {DEVICE_NAME} para incluir en los mensajes de alerta datos del cliente, la instalación y el dispositivo.
- [Instalaciones](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones): Qué es una instalación en Gear Studio: el lugar físico, como una fábrica, un edificio o un almacén, donde se despliegan dispositivos y gateways.

Es el punto de partida recomendado.

- [Ejemplo integración Raspberry Pi Pico W](https://cloudstudioiot.com/es/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/ejemplo-integracion-raspberry-pi-pico-w): Ejemplo paso a paso de integración de una Raspberry Pi Pico W con Cloud Studio IoT para enviar datos, crear alarmas y armar dashboards.

- [Actions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones): Actions are sets of steps that can be executed manually or as a consequence of configured events. Once an action starts, all associated steps are executed one after another in the established order until the sequence is completed.

- [Cliente](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente): Introdução: As seções a seguir descrevem como gerenciar clientes, incluindo criação, modificação e outros conceitos relacionados. Para Editar o Cliente...
- [Termos e Condições](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/cliente/terminos-y-condiciones): Introdução: A plataforma permite criar termos e condições com texto opcional para cada cliente, especificando os termos e condições que os usuários devem aceitar para usar as aplicações com cada cliente.
- [Alertas compostos (AND/OR)](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alertas-compuestas): Como combinar até 5 condições com AND ou OR em um único alerta, como o motor as avalia e quais são os limites dos alertas compostos.
- [Alerts](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alertas): How to set up an alert on an endpoint: alert type, threshold, minimum duration and contacts, so alarms are raised when values go out of range.
- [Configuring Contacts for Notifications](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/configuracion-contactos-para-notificaciones): Who receives an alert's notifications: address book contacts and groups, individual email addresses, and phone numbers for SMS and calls.
- [Alerts and Alarms](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas): The difference between alerts and alarms in Gear Studio: alerts set thresholds on a variable and generate alarms when they are crossed.
- [Severidade de Alarmes](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/severidad-de-alarmas): Introdução: Os níveis de severidade nos alertas indicam a criticidade associada aos alarmes. Os níveis de severidade podem ser baixo, médio ou alto, conforme mostrado na imagem a seguir.
- [Variables for Alert Notifications](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/variables-para-notificaciones-de-alertas): Variables such as {CLIENT_NAME}, {FACILITY_NAME} or {DEVICE_NAME} that put client, facility and device data into alert notification messages.
- [Facilities](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones): What a facility is in Gear Studio: the physical site, such as a factory, building or warehouse, where devices and gateways are deployed.
- [Facilities](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/instalaciones): How to create, edit and delete facilities in Gear Studio, and what else you can configure for each one in the facilities section.
- [Modo de Manutenção](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/modo-mantenimiento): Como colocar uma instalação em Modo de Manutenção para pausar alarmes e regras de automação durante reparos, sem parar a chegada de dados.
- [Sectors](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/sectores): What sectors are within a facility in Gear Studio, and how to set up automation rules that apply to each of those areas on its own.
- [Tipos de Facility](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/instalaciones/tipos-de-instalacion): Os tipos de facility no contexto IoT são categorias que permitem diferenciar e agrupar dados de acordo com a natureza e função dos ambientes físicos onde os dispositivos e gateways conectados são implantados...
- [Devices and Endpoints](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints): How Gear Studio organizes each facility's infrastructure: physical devices, such as sensors or gateways, and their endpoints.
- [Create a New Contact](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/crear-un-nuevo-contacto): How to create a new contact in the Gear Studio address book with the Add button, even while the search filter is active.
- [Create a New Contact Group](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/crear-un-nuevo-grupo-de-contacto): How to create a contact group in the Gear Studio address book: the group name and its contacts, ready to use in alerts.
- [Edit a Contact](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/editar-un-contacto): How to edit or delete a contact in the Gear Studio address book from its three-dot menu, without leaving required fields empty.
- [Edit a Contact Group](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones/editar-un-grupo-de-contactos): How to edit or delete a contact group in the address book from its three-dot menu, without leaving the name empty.
- [Address Book](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/libreta-de-direcciones): The address book holds the contacts for SMS, email and voice notifications: name, company, position, email and phone numbers.
- [Security](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/seguridad): Within "Client Configuration" in the Manager panel, you will find the Security option. Here you can add users, edit them, set a password, delete them, and also suspend them.
- [Verticals](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales): Ready-to-use verticals in Gear Studio built on the most common use cases: energy monitoring, tank monitoring and asset tracking.
- [Monitoramento de Tanques](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales/monitoreo-de-tanques): A funcionalidade de monitoramento de tanques ajuda a evitar problemas custosos e perigosos detectando falhas precocemente. Cobrindo leituras em tempo real, temperatura do tanque e sistema de alarme, ela fornece aos usuários uma representação visual do conteúdo, da temperatura e do volume total presente no tanque, entre outras variáveis disponíveis.
- [Get endpoint data incrementally](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/apis-de-extraccion-de-datos/datos-de-endpoints/obtener-datos-de-endpoints-en-forma-incremental): This API allows retrieving a list of Endpoints incrementally. This enables fast updates of Endpoints without needing to retrieve the full list.
- [Endpoint](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/herramientas-low-code-scripting/referencia-de-objetos-disponibles-para-scripting/endpoint): The endpoint object represents an endpoint within a device installed in the platform. Endpoints are normally accessed through the endpoints property of the device object.
- [Global Permissions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global/permisos-globales): Cloud Studio has a global permissions system that allows establishing, for each user or group of users, the set of functionalities they have access to at the instance level.
- [Usuários Globais](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-global/seguridad-global/usuarios-globales): Os usuários globais podem ter acesso às opções de configuração nos níveis de instância e cliente. Eles também podem pertencer a um ou mais grupos globais que têm permissões globais associadas.
- [Share Dashboard - Mobile App](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/compartir-dashboard/compartir-dashboard-app-mobile): The user can use the corresponding icon to share the Dashboard. When accessing the Dashboard from the Dashboard List and editing is required, the Share option will not be enabled until edit mode is closed.
- [Share Dashboard](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/compartir-dashboard): The user can use the corresponding icon to share the Dashboard and/or download it in two formats. When accessing the Dashboard from the Dashboard List and editing is required, the Share option will not be enabled until edit mode is closed.
- [Métricas](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/metricas): Uma métrica é uma medida quantitativa usada para avaliar e monitorar o desempenho de um sistema ou dispositivo IoT em tempo real. As métricas são usadas para coletar dados que podem ser analisados para obter insights valiosos sobre o comportamento e a eficácia do dispositivo IoT.
- [Widgets](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets): The Cloud Studio platform features a series of specific widgets for branch monitoring, energy consumption, power history, consumption, weather data, and more, for use in dashboards customizable by the end user.
- [Dynamic Widget Titles](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/titulos-dinamicos-de-widgets): Widgets with dynamic titles allow customizing the information displayed on the dashboard by using variables such as {facility_desc}, {device_desc}, and {endpoint_desc}. To use them, include them in the "Title" field when creating your widget and check the "Title" checkbox to enable this feature.
- [Device Widget](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/widget-de-dispositivos): The user can use this Widget to view relevant information about a specific device, as well as data from up to 2 of its endpoints.
- [Elementos de Alarmes](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/elementos-de-alarmas): undefined
- [Elementos de Ocupação](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/elementos-de-ocupacion): undefined
- [Elementos de Snapshot](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/elementos-de-snapshot): undefined
- [Endpoint Status Image](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/endpoint-status-image): From the Views section in the Monitor panel, the user can view the states of a discrete or scalar variable associated with an endpoint. The Endpoint status image element will display the preconfigured image based on the state reported by the endpoint.
- [Endpoint Status Text](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/vistas/elementos/endpoint-status-text): From the Views section in the Monitor panel, the user can view the states of a discrete or scalar variable associated with an endpoint. The Endpoint status text element will display the preconfigured value based on the state reported by the endpoint.

- [Editar Script Comum](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/scripts-comunes/editar-script-comun): Na seção geral de Scripts Comuns, selecione os três pontos no lado direito da tela
- [Excluir Script Comum](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/scripts-comunes/eliminar-script-comun): Na seção geral de Scripts Comuns, selecione os três pontos no lado direito da tela. O usuário deve Confirmar ou Cancelar a ação solicitada.
- [Common Scripts](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/scripts-comunes): This module allows working with Common Scripts within the selected client, to fulfill the function of reusing, simplifying, and reducing the code of Scripts for Devices and Actions.
- [Steps](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/pasos): When creating a new step, it is necessary to indicate the step type and whether it should continue to the next step in case of error. Additionally, the required attributes for each particular type must be completed.
- [Scripting utils](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/pasos/scripting-utils): Scripting utils is a complementary library of JavaScript functions that is part of the Cloud Studio platform and whose methods can be invoked from user-built JavaScript scripts in actions.
- [Alerts - Contacts and Contact Groups](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alarmas-flexibles/alertas-contactos-y-grupo-de-contactos): How to create an alert that notifies contacts or contact groups: sensor, trigger condition, normal condition and recipients.
- [Flexible Alarms](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/alarmas-flexibles): Flexible alarms: notifications that respect each contact's or group's time zone, working days and hours, and vacation periods.
- [Voice and SMS Services](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/servicios-de-voz-y-sms): How to enable voice call and SMS notifications, which have a cost, and what the platform shows about their configuration status.
- [Voice, SMS, and WhatsApp Services](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/alertas-y-alarmas/servicios-de-voz-y-sms/servicios-de-voz-sms-y-whatsapp): How to enable voice, SMS and WhatsApp notifications, which have a cost, and what the platform shows about their configuration status.
- [Controle de Dispositivos](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/control-de-dispositivos): Gear Studio permite controlar dispositivos que suportam atuação, como appliances, dimmers, termostatos, controladores de cortinas e muito mais.
- [Devices](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos): Devices are the first level of a facility's infrastructure. They typically correspond to physical devices such as sensors, gateways, dimmers, actuators, thermostats, etc.
- [Create an endpoint](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/crear-un-endpoint): IMPORTANT: as a general rule, endpoints can only be created on devices that correspond to user-defined device models. This is because when creating devices that correspond to models built into Gear Studio, the platform automatically creates all necessary endpoints.
- [Endpoint tagging](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/endpoint-tagging): The goal of this feature is to allow dashboard definitions that can be used across multiple facilities, or even different clients, without the need to create independent copies.
- [Endpoints](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints): A device can have multiple sensors, functions, or channels. For example, a dimmer capable of controlling four light circuits can be said to have four distinct functions or "channels". When a user interacts with the device, they are actually interacting with one of those channels, not the entire device.
- [Usuários](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/seguridad/usuarios): Os usuários pertencem a um ou mais grupos que têm permissões associadas. Dessa forma, é possível criar grupos com acesso exclusivo a determinadas seções e não a outras...
- [Permissions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/seguridad/usuarios/permisos): How the Cloud Studio IoT permission system works: what each user or group can use, and how to grant or deny each permission.
- [Energy Monitoring](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales/monitoreo-de-energia): The energy monitoring vertical: circuits with their hierarchy, phase and consumption category, meters, electrical variables, dashboards and alerts.
- [Asset Tracking Filters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales/seguimiento-de-activos/filtros-de-seguimiento-de-activos): How to filter what the asset tracking screen shows, which options exist and which depend on the instance's configuration.
- [Rastreamento de Ativos](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/verticales/seguimiento-de-activos): Introdução: O rastreamento de ativos permite acessar dados em tempo real da sua frota usando análises detalhadas que podem ser compartilhadas com seus colaboradores. Isso significa que você terá total confiança de que seus recursos estão sendo bem utilizados e distribuídos.
- [Widgets with Comfort Zone](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/widgets-beta/widgets-con-zona-de-confort): The Cloud Studio platform features a series of specific widgets for branch monitoring, energy consumption, power history, consumption, weather data, and more, for use in dashboards configurable by the end user.
- [Custom Filters](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/filtros-personalizados): Within the History Widget, you can use the Custom Filters option to adapt the view according to your needs. This feature allows you to select from different preloaded filters and apply them to refine the displayed information.
- [History - Aggregation](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-agregacion): Another feature of the history and comparative history widgets is the ability to view aggregated (grouped) measurements through different calculations.
- [History - Granularity](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-granularidad): State granularity is a feature that allows users to adjust the level of detail at which device state measurements are presented. This control over granularity provides crucial flexibility for monitoring, as users can choose how data is presented based on the context and analysis needs.
- [History - Grid](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-grilla): The platform includes predefined widgets that facilitate data presentation in dashboards. Among them are the history and comparative history widgets. They allow viewing the evolution of Endpoint measurements over time.
- [Histórico — Tempo de Desconexão](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos/historicos-tiempo-de-desconexion): Há ocasiões em que um dispositivo se desconecta, mas as medições continuam sendo geradas. Quando o dispositivo se reconecta, as medições armazenadas desse dispositivo são automaticamente sincronizadas com o sistema, permitindo que o usuário veja a sequência completa de dados sem intervenção manual.
- [History](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/monitor/dashboards/widgets/historicos): Line chart showing the variation of an endpoint variable type over time. In endpoint history charts, the user can enter minimum and maximum values to define the Y-axis ranges, as well as modify the variable names associated with the Y-axis titles.
- [Environment](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/pasos/scripting-objects-methods-and-properties/environment): O objeto environment (env) é o ponto de entrada para o contexto no qual existem outros objetos que representam entidades de negócio na plataforma, como a facility, dispositivos e endpoints, permitindo o acesso aos seus métodos e propriedades no desenvolvimento de scripts de ação.
- [Facility](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/pasos/scripting-objects-methods-and-properties/facility): Propriedades e métodos do objeto Facility disponíveis para scripting em ações.
- [Objetos, métodos e propriedades de scripting](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/acciones/pasos/scripting-objects-methods-and-properties): Nestas páginas você encontrará o guia dos objetos, suas propriedades e métodos que estão disponíveis para desenvolver scripts de ação.
- [Batch Device Creation](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/dispositivos/creacion-masiva-de-dispositivos-batch): The batch device creation feature allows users to efficiently load multiple devices using a CSV file. This tool is especially useful for large-scale installations, as it avoids manual entry one by one, even allowing you to combine different device models in a single file.
- [Devices](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/dispositivos): How to create a device in Gear Studio and which models it supports: those built into the platform and user-defined ones.
- [Device Model Promotion](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/dispositivos/promocion-de-modelo-de-dispositivo): On the platform, device models can exist at the local level (specific to a client) or at the global level (available to all clients in the instance). This feature allows promoting a local device model to a global model, with the goal of reusing configurations across different clients.
- [Raspberry Pi Pico W Integration Example](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/ejemplo-integracion-raspberry-pi-pico-w): By Humai Cloud Studio has all the necessary resources to offer a comprehensive solution to professionals working in the IoT field, enabling the creation of notifications and alarms, and the development of visualization panels to display real-time information about the performance and status of the IoT devices they wish to connect.
- [Helium](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/helium): The Helium integration allows the Cloud Studio IoT Platform to communicate with LoRaWAN devices using a variety of device models available on the market. This article describes the steps necessary to complete the integration.
- [Device Integration](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos): How to integrate devices with Gear Studio: have them send data to the platform and, if they support it, receive commands from it.
- [Real Time Log Broker](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/real-time-log-broker): Real Time Log Broker é um serviço que fornece visibilidade em tempo real dos eventos da plataforma relacionados ao processamento de dados dos dispositivos (Uplink) e à entrega de comandos da plataforma para os dispositivos (Downlink) na forma de entradas de log, cobrindo integrações que implementam MQTT ou HTTP API.
- [Menu Options](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/real-time-log-broker/opciones-de-menu): Once the session is open, the user can access the following functions through the control panel. Session Active - CLEAR: Clears the grid and its content, allowing new queued entries to begin logging.
- [Funções](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/real-time-log-broker/roles): Quando um usuário com permissões globais acessa a plataforma e abre o aplicativo Real Time Log Broker, ele poderá visualizar os registros monitorados da instância.
- [Clone Variable Types](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/clonar-tipos-de-variables): Introduction Variables allow us to define and determine counts or measurements of multiple states, such as temperature, time, occupancy, people flow, among others. Due to the diverse uses of variables within the platform, variable cloning was created.
- [Create a Variable Type](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/crear-un-tipo-de-variable): Go to the client, device configuration, and within it select the 'Variable Types' option, then press the Add button to configure the variable.
- [Tipos de Variáveis](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables): Introdução: Os tipos de variáveis definem as unidades de medida e o comportamento esperado de uma variável reportada à plataforma. Há certos tipos de variáveis padrão definidos por padrão na plataforma, como temperatura, umidade e pressão.
- [Local Variable Promotion](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/promocion-de-variables-locales): On the platform, variables can be defined at the client level (local) or at the global level (shared by all clients in an instance). This feature allows promoting a local variable to a global variable, facilitating its reuse across multiple contexts within the platform.
- [Global Variable Replacement](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/reemplazo-de-variable-global): This is the process by which a local variable (defined by a client) is removed and replaced by a global variable, applying this change across all devices, models, or environments where it was previously configured.

These devices do not require the use of scripting. The information on this page is useful for configuring new device models that are not natively supported by the platform.
- [Device Models](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/modelos-de-dispositivo): Introduction To facilitate device creation, the Gear Studio platform allows creating device models. Device models are primarily used to automatically describe each device's structure, its endpoints, basic properties, serial number validation rules, and much more.

This functionality is designed to allow integration with devices from a variety of manufacturers, as well as custom-built devices with Arduino, nodeMCU, Raspberry Pi, and any other platform that supports HTTP communication.
- [Flexible Data Exchange](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/http/intercambio-de-datos-flexible): Introduction Flexible data exchange is the recommended HTTP integration method when it is not possible to modify the data format sent by the device.

- [MQTT](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt): Introduction This section describes integration with the Gear Studio platform using MQTT. This functionality is designed to allow integration with devices from a variety of manufacturers, as well as custom-built devices with Arduino, nodeMCU, Raspberry Pi, and any other platform that supports MQTT communication with TLS security.
- [Flexible Data Exchange](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/integracion-de-dispositivos/mqtt/intercambio-de-datos-flexible): Introduction Flexible data exchange is the recommended MQTT integration method on the Gear Studio platform. All MQTT devices natively supported by the platform use flexible data exchange, but this method is also recommended for non-natively supported device models.
- [Expressions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones): Expressions allow performing calculations, primarily for raw data conversion in devices. What are expressions? Expressions are texts that allow evaluating data, performing calculations, and returning a single value.

A long polling mechanism is used for this purpose.

Fields are always of type string, but the content can be specified using the formats described here.
- [Formatos de Dados](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/formatos-de-datos): Ao usar as APIs via HTTP e MQTT, certos formatos de dados devem ser seguidos, conforme descrito abaixo.
- [Functions](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/funciones): Functions allow obtaining values through the transformation of others. The following is a list of functions divided into categories, according to their typical use.
- [Operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores): Operators allow creating expressions by modifying or calculating values from others, known as "operands". Depending on the type of operation to perform, and/or the data type they apply to, operators can be classified into different categories.
- [Operadores aritméticos](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-aritmeticos): Os operadores aritméticos são aplicados em operações matemáticas, e o resultado de sua aplicação é sempre um número.
- [Operadores lógicos](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-logicos): Os operadores lógicos são aplicados em operações lógicas, e o resultado de sua aplicação é sempre um valor booleano (verdadeiro / falso).
- [Operadores de string](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-para-strings): Os operadores de string são aplicados a strings de caracteres, e o resultado de sua aplicação é sempre um valor string.
- [Relational operators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/pt/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones/operadores/operadores-relacionales): Relational operators are applied in comparison operations, and the result of their application is always a boolean value (true / false).

Alle MQTT-Geräte, die nativ von der Plattform unterstützt werden, verwenden den flexiblen Datenaustausch, aber diese Methode wird auch für nicht nativ unterstützte Gerätemodelle empfohlen.
- [Ausdrücke](https://cloudstudioiot.com/de/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/endpoints/conversion-de-datos-crudos-raw/expresiones): Ausdrücke ermöglichen die Durchführung von Berechnungen, hauptsächlich für die Konvertierung von Rohdaten in Geräten. Was sind Ausdrücke? Ausdrücke sind Texte, die es ermöglichen, Daten auszuwerten, Berechnungen durchzuführen und einen einzelnen Wert zurückzugeben.

En raison des usages divers des variables au sein de la plateforme, le clonage de variables a été créé.
- [Créer un type de variable](https://cloudstudioiot.com/fr/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/crear-un-tipo-de-variable): Allez au client, à la configuration de l'appareil, et à l'intérieur sélectionnez l'option « Types de variables », puis appuyez sur le bouton Ajouter pour configurer la variable.
- [Types de variables](https://cloudstudioiot.com/fr/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables): Introduction Les types de variables définissent les unités de mesure et le comportement attendu d'une variable rapportée à la plateforme. Il existe certains types de variables standard définis par défaut sur la plateforme tels que température, humidité et pression.
- [Promotion de variables locales](https://cloudstudioiot.com/fr/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/promocion-de-variables-locales): Sur la plateforme, les variables peuvent être définies au niveau du client (locales) ou au niveau global (partagées par tous les clients d'une instance). Cette fonctionnalité permet de promouvoir une variable locale en variable globale, facilitant sa réutilisation dans plusieurs contextes au sein de la plateforme.
- [Remplacement de variable globale](https://cloudstudioiot.com/fr/docs/configuracion-del-cliente/dispositivos-y-endpoints/dispositivos/tipos-de-variables/reemplazo-de-variable-global): C'est le processus par lequel une variable locale (définie par un client) est supprimée et remplacée par une variable globale, en appliquant ce changement à tous les appareils, modèles ou environnements où elle était précédemment configurée.

## Hub

- [How to Connect an Ecowitt or Davis Weather Station](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/weather-station-iot-platform): How to get Ecowitt and Davis weather station data out of the vendor app and into an IoT platform, with the differences between the Ecowitt API v3 and the WeatherLink v2 API.
- [IoT Business Model: From Projects to Recurring Revenue](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/iot-business-model-integrators): An IoT business model built only on installation projects makes an integrator start from zero every quarter, and recurring services change that math.
- [IoT Platform API: Connect Your Data to ERP, BI, and GIS](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/iot-platform-api-integration): An IoT platform API decides whether sensor data stays on a dashboard or reaches the ERP, BI, and GIS tools where your client makes its decisions.
- [IoT Platform Cost: A Five-Year TCO Model for Integrators](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/iot-platform-cost): IoT platform cost is rarely just the license line on a quote: over five years, data, connectivity, and operations can add up to more than the software.
- [What Is NB-IoT? How Narrowband IoT Works and When to Use It](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/nb-iot): NB-IoT (narrowband IoT) explained: how it works, PSM and eDRX, NB-IoT vs LTE-M vs LoRaWAN vs Sigfox, operators in the US, Mexico and Spain, and when to use it.
- [Energy Monitoring System: How It Works and Submetering](https://cloudstudioiot.com/hub/energy-monitoring-system): What an energy monitoring system is, its parts (meters, submeters, CT clamps, Modbus, gateways), what submetering is, key metrics, and how to start one.
